define internal i64 @"std/time.floorDiv"(i64 %a, i64 %b) alwaysinline {
entry:
  %v.q = alloca i64
  %v.aNegative = alloca i1
  %v.bNegative = alloca i1
  %t1 = icmp eq i64 %b, 0
  br i1 %t1, label %panic.1, label %panic.ok.1
panic.1:
  call void @willrt_panic(ptr @.str.7, ptr @.str.8, ptr @.str.9)
  unreachable
panic.ok.1:
  %t2 = icmp eq i64 %a, -9223372036854775808
  %t3 = icmp eq i64 %b, -1
  %t4 = and i1 %t2, %t3
  br i1 %t4, label %panic.2, label %panic.ok.2
panic.2:
  call void @willrt_panic(ptr @.str.10, ptr @.str.8, ptr @.str.9)
  unreachable
panic.ok.2:
  %t5 = sdiv i64 %a, %b
  store i64 %t5, ptr %v.q
  %t6 = icmp slt i64 %a, 0
  store i1 %t6, ptr %v.aNegative
  %t7 = icmp slt i64 %b, 0
  store i1 %t7, ptr %v.bNegative
  %t8 = icmp eq i64 %b, 0
  br i1 %t8, label %panic.4, label %panic.ok.4
panic.4:
  call void @willrt_panic(ptr @.str.7, ptr @.str.11, ptr @.str.9)
  unreachable
panic.ok.4:
  %t9 = icmp eq i64 %a, -9223372036854775808
  %t10 = icmp eq i64 %b, -1
  %t11 = and i1 %t9, %t10
  br i1 %t11, label %panic.5, label %panic.ok.5
panic.5:
  call void @willrt_panic(ptr @.str.10, ptr @.str.11, ptr @.str.9)
  unreachable
panic.ok.5:
  %t12 = srem i64 %a, %b
  %t13 = icmp ne i64 %t12, 0
  %t14 = load i1, ptr %v.aNegative
  %t15 = load i1, ptr %v.bNegative
  %t16 = icmp ne i1 %t14, %t15
  %t17 = and i1 %t13, %t16
  br i1 %t17, label %if.then.3, label %if.end.3
if.then.3:
  %t18 = load i64, ptr %v.q
  %t19 = call {i64, i1} @llvm.ssub.with.overflow.i64(i64 %t18, i64 1)
  %t20 = extractvalue {i64, i1} %t19, 0
  %t21 = extractvalue {i64, i1} %t19, 1
  br i1 %t21, label %panic.6, label %panic.ok.6
panic.6:
  call void @willrt_panic(ptr @.str.10, ptr @.str.12, ptr @.str.9)
  unreachable
panic.ok.6:
  store i64 %t20, ptr %v.q
  br label %if.end.3
if.end.3:
  %t22 = load i64, ptr %v.q
  ret i64 %t22
}
